- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Dido, leader of Carthage (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Carthage
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Atlas Elephant, Replacing Horseman (Requires technology: Trade)
        - Unique Unit: Quinquereme, Replacing Trireme (Requires technology: Philosophy)
        - Unique Building: Great Cothon, Replacing Chartered Company (Requires technology: Currency)
        - Civilization Ability: +100 Gold and +25 Science when your all-time highest tradeable quantity of a Luxury increases, and +5 XP to Gold purchased Units, both scaling with Era. +100% Resource Diversity on Trade Routes. Starts with an Asamu instead of a Settler.
      - Leader: Dido
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Transnationalism: Each turn, increases the chance that a Corporate Franchise will appear in a foreign City to 15% (normally 5%). Global Franchise maximum increased by 25%, and every owned Town you have built counts as a Franchise. +2 Culture from all Corporation Offices.
      - New Deal (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): New Deal: Landmarks and Great Person improvements produce +6 of their base yield types ( Manufactory/Citadel, Town, Academy, Embassy, Holy Site, Landmark), and +2 Tourism
      - Capitalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Capitalism: 2 Specialists in each of your cities generate +1 Happiness instead of -1 Unhappiness from Urbanization. Specialists generate +1 Gold and Science.
      - Universal Healthcare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Healthcare: Receive a free Hospital in every City. +50 when a Citizen is born in any City, scaling with Era.
      - Their Finest Hour (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Their Finest Hour: All cities get +2 Air Unit Slots. Each Air Unit stationed in a city increases the City's Defense by 3. Can build B17 Bombers.
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Creative Expression (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Creative Expression: +2 Tourism from Great Works. Museums, Broadcast Towers, Opera Houses, and Amphitheaters gain +3 Golden Age Points and Culture.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Draft Registration (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Draft Registration: Military Units purchased with Gold receive full XP.
      - Arsenal of Democracy (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Arsenal of Democracy: +15% Production towards Military Units. +10 Influence with all known City-States when you expend Great People, and +40 Influence when you gift Units to City-States.
      - Avant Garde (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Avant Garde: The rate at which Great People are born is increased by 33%. -2 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Players lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Self Determination (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Self Determination: Liberating a city (including removing another civ's Sphere of Influence on a City-State through conquest) gives 15 XP to all units, 50 Influence with all City-States, and 40 Science, scaling with Era and City Population. The liberated city gains an Arsenal and 6 units.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Consulates (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.
        - * Completing a Trade Route to a City-State triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Dominance (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * All Melee Units gain the Endurance Promotion (Heal 15 HP after defeating an Enemy Unit).
        - * +1 Production in every City for every 10 Military Units in Empire.
        - * +5 Military Supply.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

GuardedBias and AfraidBias increased to 9 - must be extremely defensive and cautious. HostileBias increased to 8 to reflect our stance against Aztec/Denmark axis. Meanness slightly increased to 4 for more aggressive defensive demands. We are fighting for survival, not expansion.)
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Transnationalism (Policy)
        - Rationale: Transnationalism available in 4 turns. This Level 2 Freedom tenet is critical: 15% franchise appearance rate (3x normal), +25% global franchise max, Towns count as franchises, and +2 Culture from Corporation Offices. With International Finance Center built and Spaceflight Regulations imposing +25% gold purchase penalty on spaceship parts, I need massive gold generation. Transnationalism provides this through franchises.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Kyiv
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Removing Kyiv priority override (previously 100/95). We are at war with Kyiv due to Aztec alliance; we cannot influence them while at war. The Aztecs will likely win their Sphere of Influence proposal. Shifting resources to flippable city-states instead of wasting effort on an unreachable target during war.

We lost our +2 delegates from this former ally and must focus on protecting our remaining permanent allies (Prague and Vatican City).

Public 100 for bloc solidarity at World Congress, but private reduced to 75 to signal displeasure and caution about their diplomatic direction.

        ### Vatican City
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Vatican City is now our ONLY guaranteed permanent ally providing +2 delegates after losing Prague and others. Aztecs are actively at war with Vatican City - we must protect this at all costs. Maximum priority for delegate preservation and defensive support.

Aztecs assaulting nearby Roskilde affects regional security. Maintaining positive but reduced priority as we cannot regain alliance status immediately.
        - UpdatedTurn: 411

        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Sokoto has NEW spy mission quest against Harald Bluetooth (996 Science + 402 Influence). This is extremely valuable! Our agent Zaracas has 1494 network in Viborg (maxed) - should extract and move to Copenhagen or Ribe to complete this quest. Maximum priority to ensure AI recognizes this opportunity.
        - UpdatedTurn: 412
        
        ### Denmark
        - Public: -100

        - Rationale: Absolute hostility to Denmark. They're Aztec allies, plundered our trade routes (700g and 625g), at war with us. Their Sphere of Influence proposal (Okilis) passed. No diplomatic cooperation possible - full negative modifiers to signal permanent enemy status to AI.
        - UpdatedTurn: 412
        
        ### The Aztecs

They have Apollo complete and 73 techs vs our 68. Absolute hostility maintained - AI must treat as permanent enemy and prioritize defensive preparations and nuclear deterrent.
        - UpdatedTurn: 412
        
        ### Panama City
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: Raising Panama City relationship from 0/0 to 50/50. The spy mission quest against Montezuma (1,013 Science + 419 Influence) is worth nearly one full turn of our entire empire's science output. While Agent Baalhaan needs time to build network in Tenochtitlan (352/1494, +18/turn), signaling positive relationship ensures the AI prioritizes this mission once network is established. This quest could provide the crucial science boost needed to catch the Aztecs.
        - UpdatedTurn: 413
        
        ### Japan
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Japan declared war on us this turn after Aztecs liberated Satsuma to them. They are now part of the Aztec alliance system against us. Despite having zero military strength, they provide diplomatic cover for Aztec aggression. Setting permanent hostility to ensure AI treats them as an enemy combatant and doesn't waste resources on diplomatic overtures until war ends.
        - UpdatedTurn: 414

        - Rationale: Byzantium remains my only significant ally with 4 delegates. They have Defensive Pact and we coordinate at World Congress. However, they're Order ideology while I'm Freedom. Public max maintained for voting coordination, private opinion slightly tempered to 90 to reflect ideological differences that may cause friction on future proposals.

        # Victory Progress
        Victory Progress: current progress towards each type of victory.
        
        ## DominationVictory

              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Taoism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Dido, leader of Carthage (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Carthage
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Atlas Elephant, Replacing Horseman (Requires technology: Trade)
        - Unique Unit: Quinquereme, Replacing Trireme (Requires technology: Philosophy)
        - Unique Building: Great Cothon, Replacing Chartered Company (Requires technology: Currency)
        - Civilization Ability: +100 Gold and +25 Science when your all-time highest tradeable quantity of a Luxury increases, and +5 XP to Gold purchased Units, both scaling with Era. +100% Resource Diversity on Trade Routes. Starts with an Asamu instead of a Settler.
      - Leader: Dido
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late world Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Transnationalism: Each turn, increases the chance that a Corporate Franchise will appear in a foreign City to 15% (normally 5%). Global Franchise maximum increased by 25%, and every owned Town you have built counts as a Franchise. +2 Culture from all Corporation Offices.
      - New Deal (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): New Deal: Landmarks and Great Person improvements produce +6 of their base yield types ( Manufactory/Citadel, Town, Academy, Embassy, Holy Site, Landmark), and +2 Tourism
      - Capitalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Capitalism: 2 Specialists in each of your cities generate +1 Happiness instead of -1 Unhappiness from Urbanization. Specialists generate +1 Gold and Science.
      - Universal Healthcare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Healthcare: Receive a free Hospital in every City. +50 when a Citizen is born in any City, scaling with Era.
      - Their Finest Hour (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Their Finest Hour: All cities get +2 Air Unit Slots. Each Air Unit stationed in a city increases the City's Defense by 3. Can build B17 Bombers.
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Creative Expression (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Creative Expression: +2 Tourism from Great Works. Museums, Broadcast Towers, Opera Houses, and Amphitheaters gain +3 Golden Age Points and Culture.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Draft Registration (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Draft Registration: Military Units purchased with Gold receive full XP.
      - Arsenal of Democracy (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Arsenal of Democracy: +15% Production towards Military Units. +10 Influence with all known City-States when you expend Great People, and +40 Influence when you gift Units to City-States.
      - Avant Garde (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Avant Garde: The rate at which Great People are born is increased by 33%. -2 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Opponents lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Self Determination (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Self Determination: Liberating a city (including removing another civ's Sphere of Influence on a City-State through conquest) gives 15 XP to all units, 50 Influence with all City-States, and 40 Science, scaling with Era and City Population. The liberated city gains an Arsenal and 6 units.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Consulates (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.
        - * Completing a Trade Route to a City-State triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Dominance (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * All Melee Units gain the Endurance Promotion (Heal 15 HP after defeating an Enemy Unit).
        - * +1 Production in every City for every 10 Military Units in Empire.
        - * +5 Military Supply.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: Mobile Tactics unlocks Mechanized Infantry (strength 80) and leads to Stealth. Upgrading our infantry from strength 62 to 80 is crucial for defending Carthago Nova and our core cities against the overwhelming Aztec military.
        - Technology: Mobile Tactics
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: We are currently facing a massive gold deficit (-252 GPT) and very low happiness (47% with combat penalty). Capitalism will provide +1 Gold and Science per specialist and significantly boost happiness from specialists, helping us avoid unit disbandment and remove the combat penalty.
        - Policy: Capitalism
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - TargetID: 0
        - Rationale: Denmark is collapsing from severe unhappiness and barbarian incursions. Setting a positive private modifier will help pave the way for a peace treaty, allowing us to consolidate our defense against the Aztecs.
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 50
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - TargetID: 6
        - Rationale: Solidifying our alliance with Byzantium to ensure they remain our staunch ally and trade partner against the Aztecs.
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-persona
      - args:
        - WorkWithWillingness: 10
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - DenounceWillingness: 5
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 2
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 3
        - DiplomaticBalance: 10
        - Neediness: 2
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 10
        - WarmongerHate: 9
        - Forgiveness: 10
        - Friendliness: 10
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - MinorCivWarBias: 2
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - HostileBias: 2
        - WarBias: 2
        - Meanness: 1
        - Rationale: Adjusting persona to prioritize peaceful resolution of wars, strengthen our alliance with Byzantium, and reduce general diplomatic friction while maintaining strong city-state alliances for defense.
        - NeutralBias: 3
        - FriendlyBias: 10
        - AfraidBias: 9
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - Loyalty: 7
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 2
        - GuardedBias: 7
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - Flavors:
          - Gold: 100
          - Nuke: 10
          - NavalGrowth: 10
          - Defense: 100
          - Naval: 30
          - NavalTileImprovement: 10
          - Espionage: 50
          - TileImprovement: 60
          - Science: 100
          - UseNuke: 50
          - Infrastructure: 70
          - Happiness: 60
          - Spaceship: 100
          - GreatPeople: 50
          - Antiair: 70
          - Production: 100
          - Offense: 20
          - AirCarrier: 10
          - Culture: 10
          - CityDefense: 100
          - NavalRecon: 10
          - Mobile: 20
          - Ranged: 90
          - MilitaryTraining: 50
          - Mobilization: 100
          - Air: 90
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
      - rationale: Optimizing flavors for absolute survival against the massive Aztec threat. We prioritize Defense, Mobilization, Production, Gold, Science, and Spaceship at 100. We raise TileImprovement to 60 to clean up fallout and repair pillaged lands, raise Infrastructure to 70 to rebuild trade routes and generate gold, and raise Happiness to 60. We reduce diluting flavors like Nuke, AirCarrier, GreatPeople, and Naval to ensure our main survival priorities get maximum weight.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Opponent's Strategy**
            
            I'm assessing our current standing on turn 416 and how it aligns with the remaining 84 turns. My focus is on identifying any subtle shifts in Opponent 2's approach that might be emerging as the endgame nears.
            
            **Re-evaluating Immediate Threats**
            
            The sheer disparity in military strength with the Aztecs is alarming, necessitating a sharp pivot from my previous focus. My attention has shifted entirely to mitigating this overwhelming threat.
            
            **Assessing Victory Conditions**
            
            My attention has shifted to a multi-pronged analysis of victory conditions. While we're building the Apollo Program, I've noted the Aztecs' significant progress in domination and the potential for their diplomatic victory. Byzantium also appears close to a cultural win.
            
            **Analyzing Policy Impact**
            
            I'm currently scrutinizing the Spaceflight Regulations, recognizing its detrimental effect on our spaceship production and gold expenditure. This policy's active status requires immediate consideration, particularly with the looming vote on upcoming proposals.
            
            **Evaluating Diplomatic Landscape**
            
            The recent defeat of China by Byzantium is significant, especially as China proposed repealing "Carthaginian Sphere of Influence: Prague." This raises questions about the efficacy of proposals from defeated civilizations and requires careful monitoring.
            
            **Assessing Military Engagements**
            
            Our current military situation against the Aztecs and Denmark is dire. The stark strength disparity in Friendly Land Zone 3731, with their advanced units facing our less potent force, presents a critical challenge.
            
            **Defending Against Overwhelming Force**
            
            I'm grappling with the sheer military might of the Aztecs; their strength dwarfs ours. My focus has drastically shifted to a desperate defensive posture, assessing immediate threats and the critical state of our cities.
            
            **Evaluating Victory Paths**
            
            My primary concern is whether our spaceship production is truly viable against the looming threats. I'm scrutinizing our Science/Turn and weighing the current Grand Strategy options, considering the Aztecs' military and Byzantium's cultural progress. I'm also re-evaluating our ideology alignment, particularly with Byzantium.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Technologies**
            
            I'm focusing on our immediate technological needs, specifically on acquiring Mechanized Infantry. Unlocking this unit through Mobile Tactics seems paramount given our current defensive vulnerabilities and the strength of our adversaries. This decision is key as we're currently researching Lasers, but the next immediate step should bolster our defense.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Technologies**
            
            I'm focusing on our immediate technological needs, specifically on acquiring Mechanized Infantry. Unlocking this unit through Mobile Tactics seems paramount given our current defensive vulnerabilities and the strength of our adversaries. This decision is key as we're currently researching Lasers, but the next immediate step should bolster our defense.
            
            **Selecting Research Path**
            
            My decision is made: Mobile Tactics it is. I need to research this to upgrade our infantry and unlock Stealth for late-game defense and air dominance.
            
            **Evaluating Policy Options**
            
            I'm now reviewing the available policies. Transnationalism is the current frontrunner for the next policy slot, but I'm carefully considering the merits of New Deal, Capitalism, and Universal Healthcare alongside it, especially given our resource constraints and population growth.
            
            **Selecting Policy**
            
            I'm weighing policy options, prioritizing those that address our gold deficit and happiness crisis. Transnationalism is appealing for its potential to generate income, but I'm also considering its impact on foreign cities and any potential trade benefits.
            
            **Evaluating Policy Impacts**
            
            I am reconsidering policy choices, especially regarding Capitalism. It offers significant boosts to happiness and gold generation through specialists, which directly addresses our current critical deficits. I'm also evaluating its synergy with other potential policies and our existing situation.
            
            **Choosing Policy Path**
            
            My current focus is on resolving our significant gold deficit and happiness crisis. While Transnationalism was initially considered, Capitalism's boost to happiness and gold through specialists now seems far more impactful. I'm also re-evaluating the food boost from Civil Society and how it interacts with our policy choices.
            
            **Choosing Policy Path**
            
            My current focus is on resolving our significant gold deficit and happiness crisis. While Transnationalism was initially considered, Capitalism's boost to happiness and gold through specialists now seems far more impactful. I'm also re-evaluating the food boost from Civil Society and how it interacts with our policy choices.
            
            **Selecting Policy**
            
            My focus has sharpened on Capitalism; its specialist bonuses provide crucial gold and science, directly combatting my critical deficits. Critically, it offers a significant happiness boost, negating our current combat penalty. I'm also reviewing "Their Finest Hour" for its air unit potential.
            
            **Selecting Policy**
            
            I've decided on Capitalism as our next policy. Its specialist bonuses will provide a critical boost to gold and science, directly addressing our deficits, and crucially, it offers the happiness boost needed to negate combat penalties.
            
            **Negotiating Peace**
            
            I'm assessing our diplomatic standing and the possibility of peace. The ongoing wars with both the Aztecs and Denmark, coupled with our negative relationships, are unsustainable. I am exploring any avenues to de-escalate these conflicts.
            
            **Defending Against Aggressors**
            
            My focus is now solely on immediate survival. The sheer hostility from the Aztecs, amplified by our own nuclear strike, makes peace an impossibility. I must prioritize defending against their overwhelming force.
            
            **Leveraging Alliances**
            
            Byzantium denouncing the Aztecs is a critical development. I'm maximizing our relationship with them to pave the way for a defensive pact, aiming to counter the overwhelming Aztec threat. This requires careful management of our current modifiers, especially with defeated civs removed from the equation.
            
            **Analyzing Civilization Status**
            
            The current situation is dire; we've been defeated. The remaining active major civilizations are Denmark, the Aztecs, and Byzantium. The Aztecs' aggressive expansion is particularly notable, having absorbed Japan, Arabia, and Venice.
            
            **Analyzing Aztec Domination Drive**
            
            I'm reassessing the Aztec victory path. They now hold five capitals and are actively at war with both Denmark and us, clearly aiming for a domination victory by conquering Copenhagen and Carthage next.
            
            **Defending the Last Stand**
            
            I'm analyzing our desperate situation; Carthago Nova is at 36% health, and the Aztecs are poised to take their sixth capital. We are literally the last line of defense against their domination victory. I'm scrutinizing our defenses, focusing on holding this critical city.
            
            **Prioritizing Economic Survival**
            
            My immediate concern is our dire financial state. With a gold deficit of -252 GPT and only 411 gold remaining, bankruptcy is imminent, threatening unit disbandment and severe science penalties. My focus has entirely shifted to mitigating this critical economic collapse before it cripples our remaining progress.
            
            **Securing Immediate Funds**
            
            My current focus is on preventing imminent bankruptcy. I'm exploring all avenues for immediate gold generation, including renegotiating trade deals with our ally Byzantium and prioritizing policies like "Capitalism" for its specialist bonuses.
            
            **Utilizing Trade Routes**
            
            I've identified a critical oversight: ten unused trade routes. Immediately building Caravans and Cargo Ships will address our gold deficit, potentially generating over 400 GPT and solving our bankruptcy crisis. I'm now considering the optimal flavor settings to prioritize these vital units.
            
            **Prioritizing City Production**
            
            I'm reassessing my city production queues. Building Wonders and Archaeologists while facing bankruptcy and imminent attack is inefficient. I need to pivot to more defensive and economically viable projects, leveraging flavors to guide future city builds.
            
            **Adjusting Priority Flavors**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our flavor settings to address the imminent bankruptcy and defensive needs. Given our lack of uranium, the "Nuke" flavor is being reduced significantly to bolster "Gold," "Production," and "Defense" for immediate survival.
            
            **Adjusting Flavor Settings**
            
            I am fine-tuning our flavor settings to address imminent bankruptcy and defensive necessities. Given our limited uranium, I've significantly reduced the "Nuke" flavor to boost "Gold," "Production," and "Defense" for immediate survival. I am also prioritizing infrastructure to encourage trade routes and repairs.
            
            **Adjusting Flavor Priorities**
            
            My focus is now on an urgent flavor recalibration to combat imminent bankruptcy and repair fallout damage. I've significantly reduced "Nuke" and "AirCarrier" weights to bolster "TileImprovement" and "Infrastructure," prioritizing workers and trade units for immediate survival.
            
            **Re-evaluating Grand Strategy**
            
            My focus has shifted to re-evaluating our primary Grand Strategy. While Spaceship remains a viable path with the Apollo Program nearing completion, the Aztecs' staggering tourism output and imminent domination victory necessitate a critical assessment of whether this is still our most achievable win condition.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Persona**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our diplomatic persona to enhance survival. Given the imminent Aztec domination victory and their overwhelming power, I'm considering increasing "HostileBias," "AfraidBias," and "Friendliness" to maximize potential for peace or defensive pacts, while keeping "Boldness" and "WarBias" low.
            
            **Optimizing Diplomatic Persona**
            
            I'm fine-tuning our persona settings to maximize alliance potential against the Aztecs. Lowering HostileBias, Meanness, and GuardedBias, while significantly boosting DiplomaticBalance to 10, should foster peaceful resolutions and encourage cooperation with non-competitive civilizations.
            
            **Refining Diplomatic Persona**
            
            I'm optimizing our diplomatic persona by increasing DiplomaticBalance and FriendlyBias, aiming to foster alliances and encourage peace. Simultaneously, I'm reducing HostileBias and Meanness to present a more cooperative front, especially given the overwhelming Aztec threat.
            
            **Setting Diplomatic Persona**
            
            I am fine-tuning our persona to foster alliances and peace. By increasing DiplomaticBalance and Friendliness, and decreasing HostileBias and Meanness, I aim to present a more cooperative front, especially against the overwhelming Aztec threat, while maintaining strong city-state alliances.
            
            **Increasing Diplomatic Leverage**
            
            I'm exploring ways to improve our standing with Denmark, despite our ongoing war. By strategically adjusting private relationship modifiers, I aim to subtly shift their AI evaluation and increase the likelihood of them accepting peace, even with existing negative public sentiment.
            
            **Leveraging Diplomacy for Peace**
            
            I'm focusing on leveraging diplomacy to secure peace with Denmark, setting private relationship modifiers to encourage acceptance given their internal struggles. Peace with the Aztecs is impossible due to our nuclear strike, necessitating a fight to the death, but I'm solidifying our alliance with Byzantium to counter them.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Modifiers**
            
            I am now focusing on subtle diplomatic adjustments with Denmark. By increasing their private relationship modifier to 50 while keeping the public modifier at 0, I aim to influence their AI to accept peace, especially considering their internal struggles.
            
            **Refining Diplomatic Persona**
            
            I'm optimizing our diplomatic persona, boosting DiplomaticBalance, FriendlyBias, and Friendliness to foster alliances. Simultaneously, reducing HostileBias and Meanness presents a cooperative front, while increasing private relationship modifiers with Denmark and Byzantium secures vital peace and alliance against the overwhelming Aztec threat.
            
            **Finalizing Tool Selections**
            
            I am now consolidating my decisions. "Capitalism" and "Mobile Tactics" are confirmed policy and technology choices, respectively, and our Grand Strategy remains "Spaceship." I am preparing to execute all identified tool calls simultaneously to implement these critical adjustments efficiently.
            
            **Adjusting Priority Weights**
            
            I'm recalibrating our priority weights, significantly reducing "Nuke" and "AirCarrier" to reflect our lack of uranium and focus on essential defense. "Mobile" is also being lowered, while "TileImprovement" and "CityDefense" are being boosted to address immediate survival needs.
            
            **Setting Priority Flavors**
            
            I'm finalizing the flavor adjustments for immediate survival. Priority is now on "Defense," "Production," "Gold," "Science," and "Spaceship" at 100. I'm increasing "TileImprovement" to 60 to repair damage and raise "Happiness" to 60 due to our severe deficit.
